# Third-party components

Linkr Bee Terminal 0.1.2 is licensed under Apache-2.0.

This file is generated from the crate's `Cargo.lock`, `cargo metadata` and
`cargo tree`. It lists the normal-dependency closure across all targets
Cargo resolves; optional dependencies whose features are disabled, and
build and development dependencies, are not part of that closure.

Project repository: https://github.com/radxa/linkr-bee
Project licence: https://github.com/radxa/linkr-bee/blob/main/LICENSE

Content from these and other sources retains their original licensing,
as described below. New files from AWS-LC are made available under the Apache-2.0 license OR the ISC
license. These licenses are reproduced at the bottom of this file.
